([ramon-sg/issue-flow](https://github.com/ramon-sg/issue-flow/tree/HEAD/skills/finish-pr))

- **finish-the-job**: Completion discipline for long or multi-step work. Keep going until the whole job is done and verified instead of stopping to offer next steps, ask before deleting anything, and end with a plain-language recap that someone who saw none of the work can follow. Use for any task with more than a few steps, such as migrations, refactors, multi-file features, batches of fixes, audits with follow-up changes, or "do all of X" requests, and whenever you are about to write "Would you like me to continue?", "Next steps:", or "I'll leave the rest to you" while work remains. - **finish-the-job**: End-to-end finish protocol for any Slack thread, CLI invocation, or cron task where the user has handed off a goal. Routes to /fs (spec gen) → /f (Dark Factory loop) → drives to a verifiable conclusion (green PR with non-unit-test evidence, finished code change, or dry-run to local machine state). Never stops halfway. Loads automatically when the SOUL.md `finish-the-job` commit fires. ([jleechanorg/jleechanbrain](https://github.com/jleechanorg/jleechanbrain/tree/HEAD/skills/finish-the-job) · ★2)

- **finish-the-loop**: Closed-loop delegation — define done as an observable behavior, boot the real app, drive it like a user, fix what breaks, and verify by using it again before reporting.
